Trigger phrases include: "liquidation", "清算", "cascade", "連環清算", "health factor", "健康因子", "DeFi liquidation", "清算瀑布", "清算連鎖", "forced selling", "強制平倉", "liquidation wall", "清算牆", "underwater positions", "水下倉位", "清算風險", "cascade alert", "連鎖反應", "清算監控", "liq cascade",… ([yelunama/public-skills](https://github.com/yelunama/public-skills/tree/HEAD/SKILLS/skills-onchainCEX-strats-main/skills/liquidation-cascade-monitor))
